# Initialization Event

country_event = {
	id = turkish_debug.1
	title = turkish_debug.1.t
	desc = turkish_debug.1.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	picture = GFX_TUR_attack_PKK
	option = {
		name = turkish_debug.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_debug.1.a executed"
		set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 3 }
		set_temp_variable = { disable_elections = 1 }
		change_ruling_party_effect = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = turkish_debug.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_debug.1.a executed"
		set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 1 }
		set_temp_variable = { disable_elections = 1 }
		change_ruling_party_effect = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = turkish_debug.1.c
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_debug.1.a executed"
		set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 20 }
		set_temp_variable = { disable_elections = 1 }
		change_ruling_party_effect = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = turkish_debug.1.d1
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_debug.1.a executed"
		country_event = turkish_debug.2
	}
}

# Game-Start Economic Events (Black Wednesday)

country_event = {
	id = turkish_events.2 # The Rates Collapse
	title = turkish_events.2.t
	desc = turkish_events.2.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = turkish_events.2.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_events.2.a executed"
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 200.00 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		newline = yes
		add_stability = 0.05
	}

	option = {
		name = turkish_events.2.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_events.2.b executed"
		add_ideas = TUR_honeymoon
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = turkish_events.3 # Five Plus Five
	title = turkish_events.3.t
	desc = turkish_events.3.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = turkish_events.3.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_events.3.a executed"
		random_list = { # Keeping Suleyman for 3 more years will delay the rise of AKP and potentially prevent black wednesday
			20 = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_3_more_years_TT
			}
			80 = {
				country_event = {
					id = turkish_events.4
					days = 3
				}
			}
		}
		newline = yes
		add_stability = -0.05
		newline	= yes
		increase_corruption = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = turkish_events.3.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_events.3.b executed"
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = {
			id = turkish_events.4
			days = 3
		}
		}
	}
}

		}
	}

country_event = {
	id = turkish_events.15
	title = turkish_events.15.t
	desc = turkish_events.15.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = { has_idea = TUR_disinflation_gamble }

	option = {
		name = turkish_events.15.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_events.15.a executed"
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = TUR_disinflation_gamble
			add_idea = TUR_black_wednesday
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					TUR_lira_stability > 6
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_we_prevented_disaster_TT
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = -0.25 }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = -0.25 }
			change_relative_party_popularity = yes
			newline = yes
			add_stability = -0.10
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = turkish_events.16
	title = turkish_events.16.t
	desc = turkish_events.16.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = turkish_events.16.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_events.16.a executed"
		TUR = {
			country_event = turkish_events.17
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = SOV }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = TUR }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				has_opinion = {
					target = TUR
					value > 19
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = turkish_events.16.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_events.16.b executed"
		TUR = {
			country_event = turkish_events.18
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}
	}
}

# Greco-Turkish War Chain
country_event = {
	id = turkish_events.20
	title = turkish_events.20.t
	desc = turkish_events.20.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = turkish_events.20.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_events.20.a executed"
		add_manpower = -2
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					country_event = {
						id = turkish_events.21
						days = 1
					}
				}
				50 = {
					country_event = {
						id = turkish_events.22
						days = 1
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							TUR = {
								has_idea = NATO_member
							}
							GRE = {
								has_idea = NATO_member
							}
						}
						NATO_leave = yes
						set_country_flag = TUR_banned_from_NATO
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = turkish_events.21
	title = turkish_events.21.t
	desc = turkish_events.21.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = turkish_events.21.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_events.21.a executed"
		activate_mission = TUR_dash_across_the_strait
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					GRE = {
						has_idea = NATO_member
					}
					TUR = {
						has_idea = NATO_member
					}
				}
				remove_ideas = NATO_member
				remove_from_tech_sharing_group = NATO_Tech_Share
				# If we are the faction leader, transfer leadership before leaving
				if = {
					limit = { is_faction_leader = yes }
					# Find another NATO member to transfer leadership to
					random_other_country = {
						limit = {
							has_idea = NATO_member
							is_in_faction_with = ROOT
						}
						# Transfer faction leadership
						set_faction_leader = yes
					}
				}
				leave_faction = yes
				GRE = {
					declare_war_on = {
						target = TUR
						type = liberate_wargoal
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	id = turkish_events.23

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = turkish_events.23.a

		hidden_effect = {
			669 = { transfer_state_to = CRM }
			CRM = {
				division_template = {
					name = "Qırım Leşkeri"
					priority = 0
					regiments = {

						Militia_Bat = { x = 2 y = 1 }

					}
				}
				669 = {

					}

						division = "name = \"3rd. Qırım Müstakil\" division_template = \"Qırım Leşkeri\" start_experience_factor = 1.0 start_equipment_factor = 0.8"

					}
					create_unit = {
						division = "name = \"4th. Qırım Qoruyucıları\" division_template = \"Qırım Leşkeri\" start_experience_factor = 1.0 start_equipment_factor = 0.8"

						prioritize_location = 11649
					}
				}

					type = liberate_wargoal
				}
			}

			}
		}
		activate_mission = TUR_daring_for_crimea
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = turkish_free_syrian_army.2
	title = "Formation of the Free Syrian Army - Debug title"

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hidden = yes
	major = yes

	option = {
		name = "Debug Effect Space"
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: turkish_free_syrian_army.2.a executed"
		FSA = {
			random_core_state = {
				prioritize = { 1 }
				transfer_state_to = FSA
			}
			SYR = {
				transfer_units_fraction= {
					target = FSA
					size = 1
					stockpile_ratio = 0.5
					army_ratio = 1
					air_ratio  = 0
					navy_ratio = 0
				}

				annex_country = {
					target = FSA
				}
			}
			#Kill Syrian Units

			#Set up for FSA
			add_civil_war_debuff = yes
			ingame_startup_nations_effect = yes



			every_state = {
				limit = {
					is_core_of = SYR
				}
				add_core_of = FSA
			}

			division_template = {
				name = "FSA Battalion"

				regiments = {
					Mot_Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				}
			}

			division_template = {
				name = "FSA Brigade"

				regiments = {
					Mot_Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 0 }
					Mot_Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 1 }
					Mot_Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 2 }
				}

				support = {
					L_Engi_Comp = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				}
			}

			division_template = {
				name = "Large FSA Brigade"

				regiments = {
					Mot_Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 0 }
					Mot_Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 1 }
					Mot_Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 2 }
					Mot_Militia_Bat = { x = 1 y = 0 }
				}

				support = {
					L_Engi_Comp = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				}
			}

			division_template = {
				name = "FSA Assault Brigade"

				regiments = {
					Mot_Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 0 }
					Mot_Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 1 }
					Mot_Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 2 }
					Mot_Militia_Bat = { x = 1 y = 0 }
				}

				regimental_support = {
					armor_Comp = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				}

				support = {
					SP_Arty_Battery = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				}
			}


			566 = {
				transfer_state_to = FSA

country_event = {
	id = tur_tenders.13
	title = tur_tenders.13.name
	desc = tur_tenders.13.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_sabra

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# M60T Sabra
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.13.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.13.a"

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -20 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.14 days = 120 }
	}

	# M60-84
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.13.c
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.13.c"

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -20 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.16 days = 120 }
	}

	# M60-2000
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.13.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.13.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_stability < 0.10 }
		}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -40 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.15 days = 120 }
	}
}

###############################################################
# TANK 2000 TENDER
# (the Germany refusal keeps its 1 day timing; the choice is made
#  at the start and the tanks arrive in the stockpile when the
#  decision finishes, 100 days after it was taken. The decision's
#  remove_effect fires the matching delivery event, 10-13)
###############################################################

country_event = {
	id = tur_tenders.23
	title = tur_tenders.23.name
	desc = tur_tenders.23.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_mbt_2000_2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# Leopard 2A6 (Germany refuses)
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.23.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.23.a"

		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_germany_refused }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_done }
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_germany_refused
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = { id = tur_tenders.24 days = 7 }
		}
	}

	# Leclerc
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.23.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.23.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_stability < 0.10 }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_done
		set_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_leclerc

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -80 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}

	# Abrams
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.23.c
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.23.c"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_stability < 0.10 }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_done
		set_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_abrams

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -80 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}

	# T-84 Yatagan
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.23.d
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.23.d"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_stability < 0.10 }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_done
		set_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_t84

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -50 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}

	# Secondhand Leopard 2A4 (only after Germany refused)
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.23.e
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.23.e"

		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_germany_refused
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_stability < 0.10 }
		}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -30 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		set_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_leopard_2a4
	}

			country_event = { id = tur_tenders.34 days = 60 } # day 240
		}

			country_event = { id = tur_tenders.34 days = 120 } # day 300 design 
		}
	}

	# Domestic engine
	option = {

			base = 1
		}

		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		}

				has_country_flag = TUR_altay_otokar

					armor_type_slot = tank_composite_armor_gen3

					special_type_slot_2 = additional_mg
					special_type_slot_3 = empty
					special_type_slot_4 = tank_battlestation_2_thermal
					special_type_slot_5 = empty
					special_type_slot_6 = spaced_armor_gen3
				}
				upgrades = {
					tank_nsb_armor_upgrade = 4
					tank_nsb_engine_upgrade = 6
					tank_nsb_fire_upgrade = 2
				}
				icon = "GFX_TUR_LAND_Altay1A1"
				model = "TUR_armor_Bat_3_entity"

			}

				limit = {
					159 = { #Ankara
						is_controlled_by = ROOT
						arms_factory > 0
					}
				}
				add_equipment_production = {
					equipment = {
						type = medium_tank_chassis_2
						version_name = "Altay"
					}
					requested_factories = 1
					efficiency = 20
					industrial_manufacturer = mio:TUR_otokar_tank_manufacturer
				}
			}

		# Otokar, L44, heavy armor, domestic engine
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = TUR_altay_otokar
				has_country_flag = TUR_altay_l44
				has_country_flag = TUR_altay_heavy_armor
				has_country_flag = TUR_altay_domestic_engine
			}

			create_equipment_variant = {
				name = "Altay"
				type = medium_tank_chassis_2
				parent_version = 0
				allow_without_tech = yes
				modules = {
					main_armament_slot = tank_medium_cannon_2
					secondary_armament_slot = afv_coax_machine_gun_3
					ammunition_load_slot = anti_armor_main_ammo_2
					turret_type_slot = tank_base_tank_turret
					suspension_type_slot = tank_torsion_bar_suspension_medium

					armor_type_slot = tank_composite_armor_gen3
					engine_type_slot = tank_diesel_engine_gen4

country_event = {
	id = tur_tenders.128
	title = tur_tenders.128.name
	desc = tur_tenders.128.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_Develop_Domestic_Utility_Tender_Tuzla

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# Defender MLR41 - delivered after 100 days
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.128.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.128.a"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -10 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = {
			id = tur_tenders.131
			days = 100
		}
	}

	# Opens the Kaya / Cobra choice
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.128.c
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.128.c"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -2.5 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = tur_tenders.129
	}

	# Opens the ISV / Unimog choice
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.128.d
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.128.d"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -2.5 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = tur_tenders.130
	}
}

		}

			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -10 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.51 days = 100 }

		set_country_flag = TUR_mrap_tender_mrap_done
		hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = tur_tenders.46 } }
	}
}


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# HIDDEN ROUTER
# ------------------------------------------------------------
country_event = {
	id = tur_tenders.46
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = TUR_mrap_tender_light_done
				has_country_flag = TUR_mrap_tender_mrap_done
			}
			set_country_flag = TUR_mrap_tender_done
			clr_country_flag = TUR_mrap_tender_light_done
			clr_country_flag = TUR_mrap_tender_mrap_done
			clr_country_flag = TUR_mrap_tender_started
		}
		else = {
			country_event = { id = tur_tenders.43 days = 10 }
		}
	}
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. HIDDEN ROUTER: back to the hub (10 days later), or finish
# ------------------------------------------------------------
country_event = {
	id = tur_tenders.66
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = TUR_mrap3_tender_mrap_done
				has_country_flag = TUR_mrap3_tender_utility_done
			}
			set_country_flag = TUR_mrap3_tender_done
			clr_country_flag = TUR_mrap3_tender_mrap_done
			clr_country_flag = TUR_mrap3_tender_utility_done
			clr_country_flag = TUR_mrap3_tender_started
		}
		else = {
			country_event = { id = tur_tenders.63 days = 10 }
		}
	}
}

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.37 days = 100 }
	}

	# Korkut 140/35
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.36.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.36.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_stability < 0.10 }
		}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -20 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.38 days = 100 }
	}
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# DAY 0: PLATFORM
# ------------------------------------------------------------
country_event = {
	id = tur_tenders.39
	title = tur_tenders.39.name
	desc = tur_tenders.39.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_gurz_burc

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# Gürz
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.39.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.39.a"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_gurz

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -20 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.40 days = 40 }
	}

	# Burç
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.39.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.39.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_burc

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -10 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.40 days = 40 }
	}
}

			limit = {
				has_country_flag = TUR_burc
				has_country_flag = TUR_gurz_burc_std_gun
				has_country_flag = TUR_gurz_burc_srm
			}

			create_equipment_variant = {
				name = "Burç"
				type = medium_tank_aa_chassis_3
				parent_version = 1
				allow_without_tech = yes
				modules = {
					main_armament_slot = spaa_autocannon_3
					reload_type_slot = sixteen_missile_pack
					ammunition_load_slot = proximity_fuze_autocannon_ammo_3
					turret_type_slot = tank_base_tank_turret
					suspension_type_slot = tank_wheeled_suspension_medium
					engine_type_slot = tank_diesel_engine_gen3
					special_type_slot_4 = spaa_radar_gen3
					armor_type_slot = afv_alum_armor_gen2
				}
				icon = "GFX_TUR_LAND_Burc_A3"
				model = "TUR_armor_Bat_3_entity"
				design_team = mio:TUR_roketsan_materiel_manufacturer
				upgrades = {
					tank_nsb_engine_upgrade = 4
					tank_nsb_fire_upgrade = 2
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					159 = { #Ankara
						is_controlled_by = ROOT
						arms_factory > 0
					}
				}
				add_equipment_production = {
					equipment = {
						type = medium_tank_aa_chassis_3
						version_name = "Burç"
					}
					requested_factories = 1
					efficiency = 20
					industrial_manufacturer = mio:TUR_roketsan_materiel_manufacturer
				}
			}
		}

		# tidy up the choice flags
		clr_country_flag = TUR_gurz_burc_heavy_gun
		clr_country_flag = TUR_gurz_burc_std_gun
		clr_country_flag = TUR_gurz_burc_mrm
		clr_country_flag = TUR_gurz_burc_srm
	}

	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.42.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.42.a"
	}
}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -2.5 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

	}

	# Turret-mounted ATGM
	option = {

		ai_chance = {
			base = 6
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_acv15_aapc_atgm_yes

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -5 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.4 days = 30 }
	}

# DAY 0 - Tender: Otokar Arma vs FNSS Pars III
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
country_event = {
	id = tur_tenders.136
	title = tur_tenders.136.name
	desc = tur_tenders.136.desc
	picture = GFX_turkey_afv_tender

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# Otokar Arma
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.136.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.136.a"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_afv_tender_arma

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -20 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		# Design is added after 120 days
		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.137 days = 120 }
	}

	# FNSS Pars III
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.136.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.136.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_stability < 0.10 }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_afv_tender_pars3

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -15 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		# Design is added after 120 days
		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.137 days = 120 }
	}
}

					}
					requested_factories = 1
					efficiency = 20
					industrial_manufacturer = mio:TUR_fnss_tank_manufacturer
				}
			}

			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = medium_tank_flame_chassis_2
				variant_name = "FNSS Pars III ZMA"
				amount = 20
				producer = TUR
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = medium_tank_amphibious_chassis_2
				variant_name = "FNSS Pars III ZPT"
				amount = 20
				producer = TUR
			}

			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -20 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}

		# Clean up choice flags
		clr_country_flag = TUR_afv_tender_arma
		clr_country_flag = TUR_afv_tender_pars3
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------
# DAY 0 - Choose vehicle: FNSS Pars Alpha ZMA 120 HT / Otokar Arma XC-8 120mm / BMC Altug Hitfact 120mm
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
country_event = {
	id = tur_tenders.84
	title = tur_tenders.84.name
	desc = tur_tenders.84.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_ynzma_lt_1

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# FNSS Pars Alpha ZMA 120 HT
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.84.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.84.a"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_ynzma2_alpha

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -15 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.85 days = 30 }
	}

	# Otokar Arma XC-8 120mm
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.84.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.84.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_ynzma2_arma

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -20 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.85 days = 30 }
	}

	# BMC Altug Hitfact 120mm
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.84.c
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.84.c"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
			modifier = { factor = 2 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_ynzma2_altug

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -15 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.85 days = 30 }
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------
# DAY 0 - Choose vehicle: FNSS Pars Alpha ZMA / Otokar Arma 2 / BMC Altug
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
country_event = {
	id = tur_tenders.89
	title = tur_tenders.89.name
	desc = tur_tenders.89.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_ynzma_apc_1

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# FNSS Pars Alpha ZPT
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.89.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.89.a"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_ynzma3_alpha

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -10 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.90 days = 30 }
	}

	# Otokar Arma 2 ZPT
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.89.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.89.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_ynzma3_arma2

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -12 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.90 days = 30 }
	}

	# BMC Altug ZPT
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.89.c
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.89.c"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
			modifier = { factor = 2 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_ynzma3_altug

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -8 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.90 days = 30 }
	}
}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 6
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_ynzma3_engine_hybrid

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -10 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.93 days = 30 }
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------
# DAY 0 - Choose vehicle: FNSS Kaplan IFV / Otokar Tulpar IFV
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
country_event = {
	id = tur_tenders.94
	title = tur_tenders.94.name
	desc = tur_tenders.94.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_ynzma_ifv_1

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# FNSS Kaplan IFV
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.94.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.94.a"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_ynzma4_kaplan

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -10 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.95 days = 30 }
	}

	# Otokar Tulpar IFV
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.94.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.94.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_ynzma4_tulpar

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -8 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.95 days = 30 }
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------
# DAY 0 - Choose vehicle: FNSS Kaplan LT / Otokar Tulpar LT
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
country_event = {
	id = tur_tenders.100
	title = tur_tenders.100.name
	desc = tur_tenders.100.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_ynzma_tlt_1

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# FNSS Kaplan LT
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.100.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.100.a"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_ynzma5_kaplan

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -6 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.101 days = 30 }
	}

	# Otokar Tulpar LT
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.100.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.100.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_ynzma5_tulpar

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -5 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.101 days = 30 }
	}
}

###############################################################
# SPG TENDER
# (choice is instant at day 0, delivery comes 120 days later)
###############################################################

country_event = {
	id = tur_tenders.9
	title = tur_tenders.9.name
	desc = tur_tenders.9.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_spg

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# Korean K9 Thunder (license production, becomes T-155 Firtina)
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.9.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.9.a"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -30 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.10 days = 120 }
	}

	# German PzH 2000 (very capable, very expensive)
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.9.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.9.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 2
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_stability < 0.10 }
		}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -60 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.11 days = 120 }
	}

	# British AS-90 (cheaper off-the-shelf purchase, no license)
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.9.c
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.9.c"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 3
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -35 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.12 days = 120 }
	}
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# DELIVERY EVENTS (fire 120 days after the choice is made)
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# K9 Thunder -> T-155 Firtina (license production begins)
country_event = {
	id = tur_tenders.10
	title = tur_tenders.10.name
	desc = tur_tenders.10.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_firtina

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = TUR_spg_firtina

		create_equipment_variant = {
			name = "T-155 Firtina"
			type = medium_tank_artillery_chassis_2
			parent_version = 1
			modules = {
				main_armament_slot = art_med_gun_gen2
			   reload_type_slot = automatic_loading
			   ammunition_load_slot = artillery_medium_ammo_2
			   turret_type_slot = tank_base_tank_turret
				suspension_type_slot = tank_pneumatic_suspension_medium
				engine_type_slot = tank_diesel_engine_gen3
				special_type_slot_1 = empty
				special_type_slot_2 = fire_ammo_2
				special_type_slot_3 = fire_ammo_2
				special_type_slot_4 = tank_battlestation_3
				special_type_slot_5 = multiple_rounds_medium_gen1
			}
			upgrades = {
				tank_nsb_fire_upgrade = 2
			}
			icon = "GFX_TUR_LAND_T_155_A1"
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				159 = { #Ankara
					is_controlled_by = ROOT
					arms_factory > 0
				}
			}
			add_equipment_production = {
				equipment = {
					type = medium_tank_artillery_chassis_2
					version_name = "T-155 Firtina"
				}
				requested_factories = 1
				efficiency = 20
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.10.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.10.a"
	}
}

	desc = tur_tenders.11.desc

			type = medium_tank_artillery_chassis_2

			producer = GER
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.11.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.11.a"
	}
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------

	id = tur_tenders.109
	title = tur_tenders.109.name
	desc = tur_tenders.109.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_kaan_1

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# Medium aircraft
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.109.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.109.a"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_kaan_medium

		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -20 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.110 days = 200 }
	}

	# Lightweight aircraft
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.109.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.109.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_kaan_light

			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -10 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes

	}

	# American F110-129
	option = {
		name = tur_tenders.111.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tenders.111.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_kaan_f110

		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -20 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}

		country_event = { id = tur_tenders.112 days = 200 }
	}
}

add_namespace = TUR_pkk

# The PKK's yearly campaign. Fired at game start and rescheduled by TUR_pkk.2.
country_event = {
	id = TUR_pkk.1
	title = TUR_pkk.1.t
	desc = {
		text = TUR_pkk.1.d_known
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				agency_upgrade_number > 3
				check_variable = { TUR_PKK_hint_first = 0 }
			}
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = TUR_pkk.1.d_unclear
		trigger = {
			NOT = { agency_upgrade_number > 3 }
			check_variable = { TUR_PKK_hint_first > 0 }
		}
	}
	picture = GFX_TUR_PKK_generic

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_PKK_gone }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_pkk_breakaway }
	}

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			clear_variable = TUR_PKK_campaign
			TUR_pkk_roll_campaign = yes
			set_variable = { TUR_PKK_campaign = TUR_pkk_rolled_campaign }
			TUR_pkk_roll_campaign = yes
			set_variable = { TUR_PKK_hint_first = { value = TUR_PKK_campaign min = TUR_pkk_rolled_campaign } }
			set_variable = { TUR_PKK_hint_second = { value = TUR_PKK_campaign max = TUR_pkk_rolled_campaign } }
			country_event = { id = TUR_pkk.10 days = 100 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = TUR_pkk.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: TUR_pkk.1.a executed"
		add_command_power = -20
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_pkk_stance_commandos_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { TUR_PKK_stance = 1 }
			country_event = { id = TUR_pkk.2 days = 214 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { factor = 3 check_variable = { TUR_PKK_campaign = 1 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.2 command_power < 20 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = TUR_pkk.1.b
		trigger = { check_variable = { TUR_kurdish_grievance > 49 } }
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: TUR_pkk.1.b executed"
		add_stability = -0.02
		set_temp_variable = { TUR_grievance_change = 5 }
		TUR_pkk_change_grievance = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_pkk_stance_curfews_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { TUR_PKK_stance = 2 }
			country_event = { id = TUR_pkk.2 days = 214 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { factor = 3 check_variable = { TUR_PKK_campaign = 2 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_stability < 0.3 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = TUR_pkk.1.c
		trigger = { has_global_flag = GLOBAL_syrian_civil_war_started }
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: TUR_pkk.1.c executed"
		medium_expenditure = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_pkk_stance_border_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { TUR_PKK_stance = 3 }
			country_event = { id = TUR_pkk.2 days = 214 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { factor = 3 check_variable = { TUR_PKK_campaign = 3 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = TUR_pkk.1.d
		trigger = { has_idea = TUR_PKK_insurgency_ceasefire }
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: TUR_pkk.1.d executed"
		add_political_power = -35
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_pkk_stance_talks_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { TUR_PKK_stance = 4 }
			country_event = { id = TUR_pkk.2 days = 214 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { factor = 3 check_variable = { TUR_PKK_campaign = 4 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
		}
	}
}

# Autumn result of the campaign, then yearly recruitment from Kurdish Grievance.
country_event = {
	id = TUR_pkk.2
	title = TUR_pkk.2.t
	desc = {
		text = TUR_pkk.2.d_hit
		trigger = { check_variable = { TUR_PKK_stance = TUR_PKK_campaign } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = TUR_pkk.2.d_miss
		trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { TUR_PKK_stance = TUR_PKK_campaign } } }
	}
	picture = GFX_TUR_attack_PKK

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_PKK_gone }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_pkk_breakaway }
	}

	option = {
		name = TUR_pkk.2.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: TUR_pkk.2.a executed"
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { TUR_PKK_stance = TUR_PKK_campaign } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { TUR_PKK_campaign = 4 } }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_pkk_strength_change = -5 }
				TUR_pkk_change_strength = yes
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_grievance_change = -10 }
				TUR_pkk_change_grievance = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { TUR_PKK_campaign = 2 } }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_pkk_damage = 15 }
				TUR_pkk_military_damage = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_pkk_damage = 12 }
				TUR_pkk_military_damage = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_stability = -0.03
			set_temp_variable = { TUR_pkk_strength_change = 4 }
			TUR_pkk_change_strength = yes
			set_temp_variable = { TUR_grievance_change = 3 }
			TUR_pkk_change_grievance = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { TUR_PKK_campaign = 4 } } }
			TUR_pkk_end_ceasefire = yes
		}
		TUR_pkk_yearly_recruitment = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = TUR_pkk_campaign_revealed
			country_event = { id = TUR_pkk.1 days = 151 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = TUR_pkk_peace_open
					NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_pkk_peace_locked }
				}
				random_list = {
					70 = { country_event = { id = TUR_pkk.7 days = 30 } }
					30 = {
						modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { TUR_PKK_strength > 49 } }
						modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { TUR_pkk_peace_stage = 0 } }
						country_event = { id = TUR_pkk.8 days = 30 }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = TUR_village_guards }
				random_list = {
					20 = { country_event = { id = TUR_pkk.9 days = 60 } }
					80 = { }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	}
}

	id = TUR_pkk.4
	title = TUR_pkk.4.t
	desc = TUR_pkk.4.d
	picture = GFX_kurd_riot

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = { NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_PKK_gone } }

	immediate = { hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = TUR_pkk_uprising_seen } }

	option = {
		name = TUR_pkk.4.a

		set_temp_variable = { TUR_pkk_damage = 30 }
		TUR_pkk_military_damage = yes
		set_temp_variable = { TUR_grievance_change = 15 }
		TUR_pkk_change_grievance = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { factor = 3 TUR_ai_keeps_the_southeast = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_stability < 0.3 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = TUR_pkk.4.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: TUR_pkk.4.b executed"
		add_political_power = -100
		set_temp_variable = { TUR_pkk_strength_change = -20 }
		TUR_pkk_change_strength = yes
		set_temp_variable = { TUR_grievance_change = -10 }
		TUR_pkk_change_grievance = yes
		TUR_pkk_start_ceasefire = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 5
			modifier = { factor = 3 has_global_flag = TUR_TURKISH_LEFT_AI_FOCUS }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
		}
	}
}

# Strength at 100 after an earlier uprising, or with Grievance at 75 or more: the PKK breaks away and goes to war.
country_event = {
	id = TUR_pkk.5
	title = TUR_pkk.5.t
	desc = TUR_pkk.5.d
	picture = GFX_Kurdish_autonomous1

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_PKK_gone }
		NOT = { country_exists = PKK }
	}

	option = {
		name = TUR_pkk.5.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: TUR_pkk.5.a executed"
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_pkk_breakaway_tt
		set_country_flag = TUR_pkk_breakaway
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { TUR_PKK_strength = 50 }
			162 = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_owned_by = ROOT }
					transfer_state_to = PKK
				}
			}
			935 = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_owned_by = ROOT }
					transfer_state_to = PKK
				}
			}
			1205 = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_owned_by = ROOT }
					transfer_state_to = PKK
				}
			}
			PKK = {
				division_template = {
					name = "HPG Guerrillas"
					regiments = {
						Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 0 }
						Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 1 }
						Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 2 }
						Militia_Bat = { x = 1 y = 0 }
						Militia_Bat = { x = 1 y = 1 }
						Militia_Bat = { x = 1 y = 2 }
					}
					priority = 2
				}
				every_owned_state = {
					create_unit = {
						division = "name = \"HPG Brigade\" division_template = \"HPG Guerrillas\" start_experience_factor = 0.3"
						owner = PKK
						count = 3
					}
				}
				declare_war_on = { target = ROOT type = annex_everything }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Fired by TUR_deal_with_ocalan.
country_event = {
	id = TUR_pkk.6
	title = TUR_pkk.6.t
	desc = TUR_pkk.6.d
	picture = GFX_kurd_event

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_PKK_gone }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_pkk_peace_locked }
	}

	option = {
		name = TUR_pkk.6.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: TUR_pkk.6.a executed"
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_pkk_peace_locked_tt
		set_country_flag = TUR_pkk_peace_locked
		add_war_support = 0.05
		set_temp_variable = { TUR_pkk_strength_change = 10 }
		TUR_pkk_change_strength = yes
		set_temp_variable = { TUR_grievance_change = 15 }
		TUR_pkk_change_grievance = yes
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.EU_member
			tooltip = TT_ALL_EU_MEMBER_NATIONS_GAIN
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = medium_decrease }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 2
			modifier = { factor = 5 has_global_flag = TUR_GREY_WOLVES_AI_FOCUS }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = TUR_pkk.6.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: TUR_pkk.6.b executed"
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_pkk_peace_open_tt
		set_country_flag = TUR_pkk_peace_open
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { factor = 2 has_global_flag = TUR_HISTORICAL_AI_FOCUS }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = TUR_pkk.6.c
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: TUR_pkk.6.c executed"
		set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -5 }
		change_the_military_opinion = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_pkk_peace_open_tt
		set_country_flag = TUR_pkk_peace_open
		TUR_pkk_peace_step = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 5
			modifier = { factor = 4 has_global_flag = TUR_TURKISH_LEFT_AI_FOCUS }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
		}
	}
}

# A rung of the Solution Process, fired from TUR_pkk.2 while the process is open.

		text = TUR_pkk.7.d1
		trigger = { check_variable = { TUR_pkk_peace_stage = 0 } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = TUR_pkk.7.d2
		trigger = { check_variable = { TUR_pkk_peace_stage = 1 } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = TUR_pkk.7.d3
		trigger = { check_variable = { TUR_pkk_peace_stage = 2 } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = TUR_pkk.7.d4
		trigger = { check_variable = { TUR_pkk_peace_stage = 3 } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = TUR_pkk.7.d5
		trigger = { check_variable = { TUR_pkk_peace_stage = 4 } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = TUR_pkk.7.d6
		trigger = { check_variable = { TUR_pkk_peace_stage = 5 } }
	}
	picture = GFX_kurd_rally

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_PKK_gone }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_pkk_peace_locked }
		check_variable = { TUR_pkk_peace_stage < 6 }
	}

	option = {
		name = TUR_pkk.7.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: TUR_pkk.7.a executed"
		TUR_pkk_peace_step = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { factor = 3 has_global_flag = TUR_TURKISH_LEFT_AI_FOCUS }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_global_flag = TUR_HISTORICAL_AI_FOCUS }
			modifier = { factor = 0.2 has_global_flag = TUR_GREY_WOLVES_AI_FOCUS }
			modifier = { factor = 0.3 has_global_flag = TUR_KEMALIST_GUARD_AI_FOCUS }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_stability < 0.3 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = TUR_pkk.7.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_pkk_peace_stall_tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = 3
		}
	}
}

# A spoiler attack between rungs of the Solution Process.
country_event = {
	id = TUR_pkk.8
	title = TUR_pkk.8.t
	desc = {
		text = TUR_pkk.8.d_bombing
		trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = GLOBAL_syrian_civil_war_started } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = TUR_pkk.8.d_kobani
		trigger = { has_global_flag = GLOBAL_syrian_civil_war_started }
	}
	picture = GFX_pkk_ambush

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_PKK_gone }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_pkk_peace_locked }
		check_variable = { TUR_pkk_peace_stage > 0 }
	}

	option = {
		name = TUR_pkk.8.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: TUR_pkk.8.a executed"
		add_stability = -0.03
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_pkk_spoiler_absorb_tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { factor = 2 has_global_flag = TUR_TURKISH_LEFT_AI_FOCUS }
			modifier = { factor = 0.3 has_stability < 0.3 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = TUR_pkk.8.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: TUR_pkk.8.b executed"
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_pkk_peace_setback_tt
		subtract_from_variable = { TUR_pkk_peace_stage = 1 }
		TUR_pkk_end_ceasefire = yes
		set_temp_variable = { TUR_pkk_damage = 10 }
		TUR_pkk_military_damage = yes
		set_temp_variable = { TUR_grievance_change = 5 }
		TUR_pkk_change_grievance = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 5
			modifier = { factor = 3 has_global_flag = TUR_GREY_WOLVES_AI_FOCUS }
			modifier = { factor = 3 has_global_flag = TUR_KEMALIST_GUARD_AI_FOCUS }
		}
	}
}

# Village guard abuse scandal, rolled each autumn while the guards serve.
country_event = {
	id = TUR_pkk.9
	title = TUR_pkk.9.t
	desc = TUR_pkk.9.d
	picture = GFX_pkk_munic

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_PKK_gone }
		has_idea = TUR_village_guards
	}

	option = {
		name = TUR_pkk.9.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: TUR_pkk.9.a executed"
		set_temp_variable = { TUR_grievance_change = 8 }
		TUR_pkk_change_grievance = yes
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.EU_member
			tooltip = TT_ALL_EU_MEMBER_NATIONS_GAIN
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = small_decrease }
		}
	}
}

# Mid-season report, 100 days after the spring campaign event. Reveals the campaign so a wrong guess can be fixed with TUR_pkk_redeploy.
country_event = {
	id = TUR_pkk.10
	title = TUR_pkk.10.t
	desc = {
		text = TUR_pkk.10.d1
		trigger = { check_variable = { TUR_PKK_campaign = 1 } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = TUR_pkk.10.d2
		trigger = { check_variable = { TUR_PKK_campaign = 2 } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = TUR_pkk.10.d3
		trigger = { check_variable = { TUR_PKK_campaign = 3 } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = TUR_pkk.10.d4
		trigger = { check_variable = { TUR_PKK_campaign = 4 } }
	}
	picture = GFX_pkk_ambush

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_PKK_gone }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_pkk_breakaway }
	}

	immediate = { hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = TUR_pkk_campaign_revealed } }

	option = {
		name = TUR_pkk.10.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: TUR_pkk.10.a executed"
		add_command_power = -10
		set_temp_variable = { TUR_pkk_damage = 4 }
		TUR_pkk_military_damage = yes
		set_temp_variable = { TUR_grievance_change = 2 }
		TUR_pkk_change_grievance = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { factor = 2 has_global_flag = TUR_GREY_WOLVES_AI_FOCUS }
			modifier = { factor = 0.2 command_power < 10 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = TUR_pkk.10.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: TUR_pkk.10.b executed"
		set_temp_variable = { TUR_pkk_strength_change = 2 }
		TUR_pkk_change_strength = yes
		set_temp_variable = { TUR_grievance_change = -3 }
		TUR_pkk_change_grievance = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { factor = 2 has_global_flag = TUR_TURKISH_LEFT_AI_FOCUS }
		}
	}
}
